Database Schema:

Table daily_prices {
  # Partitioned table for daily stock prices and technical indicators
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - symbol
  #   - data_source
  id: bigint (not null)
  symbol: character varying (primary key) (not null) (sticky)
  trade_date: date (primary key) (not null)
  open_price: numeric
  high_price: numeric
  low_price: numeric
  close_price: numeric
  adj_close: numeric
  volume: bigint
  rsi: numeric
  moving_avg_20: numeric
  moving_avg_50: numeric
  moving_avg_200: numeric
  volatility: numeric
  data_source: character varying (sticky)
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  updated_at: timestamp without time zone
}

Table securities {
  # Master table for all securities/stocks with company information
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - company_name
  #   - symbol
  symbol: character varying (primary key) (not null) (sticky)
  company_name: character varying (not null) (sticky)
  sector: character varying
  industry: character varying
  market_cap: bigint
  exchange: character varying
  country: character varying
  currency: character varying
  is_active: boolean
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  updated_at: timestamp without time zone
}

Table daily_prices_2023_01 {
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - symbol
  #   - data_source
  id: bigint (not null)
  symbol: character varying (primary key) (not null) (sticky)
  trade_date: date (primary key) (not null)
  open_price: numeric
  high_price: numeric
  low_price: numeric
  close_price: numeric
  adj_close: numeric
  volume: bigint
  rsi: numeric
  moving_avg_20: numeric
  moving_avg_50: numeric
  moving_avg_200: numeric
  volatility: numeric
  data_source: character varying (sticky)
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  updated_at: timestamp without time zone
}

Table financial_statements {
  # Quarterly and annual financial statement data
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - data_source
  id: bigint (primary key) (not null)
  symbol: character varying (not null)
  fiscal_year: integer (not null)
  fiscal_quarter: integer
  period_end_date: date
  revenue: bigint
  cost_of_revenue: bigint
  gross_profit: bigint
  operating_expenses: bigint
  operating_income: bigint
  net_income: bigint
  total_assets: bigint
  current_assets: bigint
  total_liabilities: bigint
  current_liabilities: bigint
  shareholders_equity: bigint
  total_debt: bigint
  cash_and_equivalents: bigint
  earnings_per_share: numeric
  book_value_per_share: numeric
  shares_outstanding: bigint
  pe_ratio: numeric
  pb_ratio: numeric
  debt_to_equity: numeric
  roe: numeric
  roa: numeric
  data_source: character varying (sticky)
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  → securities via symbol
}

Table economic_indicators {
  # Economic data from FRED (Federal Reserve Economic Data)
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - indicator_name
  #   - indicator_code
  indicator_code: character varying (primary key) (not null) (sticky)
  indicator_name: character varying (sticky)
  date: date (primary key) (not null)
  value: numeric
  unit: character varying
  frequency: character varying
  data_source: character varying
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
}

Table trades {
  # Individual trading transactions for portfolio tracking
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - trade_type
  #   - portfolio_id
  trade_id: bigint (primary key) (not null)
  symbol: character varying (not null)
  trade_timestamp: timestamp with time zone (not null)
  trade_type: character varying (sticky)
  quantity: integer (not null)
  price: numeric (not null)
  commission: numeric
  portfolio_id: character varying (sticky)
  trader_id: character varying
  order_type: character varying
  exchange: character varying
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  → securities via symbol
}

Table portfolio_holdings {
  # Current portfolio positions and performance
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - portfolio_id
  id: bigint (primary key) (not null)
  portfolio_id: character varying (not null) (sticky)
  symbol: character varying (not null)
  quantity: integer (not null)
  avg_cost: numeric
  current_price: numeric
  market_value: numeric
  unrealized_pnl: numeric
  realized_pnl: numeric
  first_purchase_date: date
  last_updated: timestamp without time zone
  → securities via symbol
}

Table options_contracts {
  # Options contracts data with Greeks for advanced analysis
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - contract_id
  #   - option_type
  contract_id: character varying (primary key) (not null) (sticky)
  underlying_symbol: character varying (not null)
  option_type: character varying (sticky)
  strike_price: numeric (not null)
  expiration_date: date (not null)
  premium: numeric
  bid_price: numeric
  ask_price: numeric
  last_price: numeric
  volume: integer
  open_interest: integer
  delta: numeric
  gamma: numeric
  theta: numeric
  vega: numeric
  rho: numeric
  implied_volatility: numeric
  trade_date: date (not null)
  data_source: character varying
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  → securities via underlying_symbol
}

Table market_data {
  # Real-time market data including bid/ask spreads
  # Primary identifiers:
  #   - data_source
  id: bigint (primary key) (not null)
  symbol: character varying (not null)
  timestamp: timestamp with time zone (not null)
  bid_price: numeric
  ask_price: numeric
  bid_size: integer
  ask_size: integer
  last_price: numeric
  volume: integer
  spread: numeric
  data_source: character varying (sticky)
  created_at: timestamp without time zone
  → securities via symbol
}
